Id ledger: 20 known (max CAP-021); new —.

Policy in force:

- **adjudication.** a unit counts as support only when adjudicated supports_claim against the cluster claim_under_test after photo and lineage review; refuted, unclear, unavailable, and missing units are context and never inflate counts.
- **coverage_gaps.** an evidence-backed missing-coverage group that meets the bar is promoted regardless of expressibility: native if a legitimate split parent exists, else migration_system_gap.
- **evidence_bar.** two_independent_human = at least two adjudicated human/gold units (same property flagged and noted); one_human_plus_independent_corroboration = one adjudicated human/gold unit plus an adjudicated gold/lead unit on a different photo and condition (same photo/condition is method corroboration); structural_exception = a single case plus a mechanically demonstrable universal catalog fact and written justification, never for a missing-coverage cluster; none => deferred_insufficient_evidence or no_change unless the outcome is refutation-backed. A reviewed refutation (a human/gold support-or-counterexample unit adjudicated refuted with photos available) never raises the bar but may ground no_change, non_catalog_action or migration_system_gap; when human/gold support units are both supported and refuted the claim must carry a conflict_note.
- **evidence_classes.** human_review (queue case with human truth) and gold_reference (gold photo finding) are human evidence; model_judge (factorized lead) is a model pointer that never satisfies the bar alone; the review session's photo/lineage adjudication is a model annotation that can refute a lead but cannot upgrade it.
- **noise_floor.** human self-consistency 80% on the v1.1 repeat arm; Terra replica disagreement 6.4%; the 2d-vs-Terra stage boundary sits inside that noise (docs/RESULT_error_attribution_20260831.md section 7).
- **non_deferred_outcomes.** native conclusions cite only adjudicated, photo-and-lineage-reviewed human/gold support and require a met bar; non-catalog, migration-system-gap and no-change conclusions cite at least one reviewed human/gold unit, support or refutation, with photos available; model-only leads, unclear, unavailable and missing rows never qualify; only deferral may rest on unavailable or unclear evidence.
- **operational_equivalence.** every cluster records mismatch_class, and the dimensions that differ whenever it is operationally_consequential; equivalence requires the same physical subject, repair, approximate scope, billability, trade/route and safety implication; a wrong subject, mechanism, severity, route or implied diagnostic obligation is consequential even under a shared broad repair label; internal catalog precision may be finer than the UI, and no item is added or split to make the UI more verbose.
- **pooling.** one semantic issue across several items is one cluster; pooling rests on shared subject + mechanism + commitment type recorded in merge_split_rationale, never on the R1/R3 stage label.
- **priority.** judge on fewer hallucinations and fewer lost real conditions; dollar effects are context only (Steven, 2026-08-21).
- **quarantine.** product-quarantined trades (from catalog metadata) are non_catalog_action / product_quarantined by rule.
- **review_binding.** each unit's adjudication is read from the pinned review row named by review_row_id (default {proposal_id}:{unit_key}, or coverage:{unit_key} for a promoted coverage unit); rows may be bound across reshaped clusters only with cluster.reshape and a per-unit transfer_note, and a reshaped cluster reads its controls from the source cluster; one reviewed row grounds exactly one cluster; adjudications are never rewritten, and packet drift after reshaping is expected because the pinned packet remains the reviewed one.
- **review_provenance.** photo/lineage review runs in a separate session against reports/catalog_audit_photo_review_packet.json and writes reports/catalog_audit_photo_review.json, pinned by sha256; diagnosis fields stay null until the results are pinned.
- **scope_direction.** catalog-first intervention (Steven, 2026-09-03): the primary candidates are catalog-owned misses of real billable conditions and consequential semantic defects; Pass 2a is frozen, so Pass-2a-owned findings are deferred with reason out_of_scope_pass_2a and hallucination cards serve only as regression controls; Pass 2d is the next optimisation target after approved catalog work, so a reachable-right-item selection failure is recorded in pass_2d_follow_ups with the catalog left unchanged.

## Native decisions proposals

### CAP-007 — Window treatments vs windows: one item conflates blinds with valances/curtains, and the window item absorbs treatment-level findings

Status `native_decisions_proposal` · outcome `native_decisions_proposal` · confidence `low` · items `dated_window_treatment_valance`, `dated_or_older_windows`

**Claim under test.** The photo shows dated, bent, partial, or damaged window blinds/treatments (not a valance, and not the window unit), and the resolved catalog item commits to a valance (dated_window_treatment_valance) or to the window itself (dated_or_older_windows).

**Cluster.** subject: window blinds and treatments; mechanism: dated, bent, or damaged blinds and treatment remnants resolved to an item committing to a valance or to the window unit itself; kinds: modernization; scenes: bedroom, living_areas; commitments: object. Kept pooled with all four reviewed units and both items; the allocated id CAP-020 is deliberately unused. The prior hypothesis that CAP-007 pools two failures is CORRECT on the facts - an over-bill lane (ordinary intact blinds recruited onto a valance-committed item) and a loss lane (real treatment-level findings landing on the window item or on nothing) - but partitioning them destroys the analysis. The two gold units at redfin_11000447 are the family's only supports_claim evidence, so every partition leaves each resulting cluster at bar `none`, and bar `none` forecloses a native_decisions_proposal. Pooled, the two lanes are the two halves of one defect and a fix needs both: the refutations plus hallucination control rc_429ba6851d09 show the item over-reaching onto blinds, g7 and positive-use control rc_7c5b9f9bb3aa show the valance/curtain concept is real and billable, and g4 shows blinds-only conditions exist with no valance at all. The claim wording is kept verbatim from the reviewed Phase B packet because all four adjudications were made against that exact string; the blinds-vs-valance framing lives in the diagnosis, not in a rewritten claim.

**Mismatch class.** `operationally_consequential`; differing dimensions subject, billability, route.

| gold:redfin_11000447:photo_004.jpg:g7 | gold_reference | support | redfin_11000447 | photo_004.jpg | supports_claim | CAP-007:gold:redfin_11000447:photo_004.jpg:g7 | — | miss_candidate |
| gold:redfin_11000447:photo_005.jpg:g4 | gold_reference | support | redfin_11000447 | photo_005.jpg | supports_claim | CAP-007:gold:redfin_11000447:photo_005.jpg:g4 | — | miss_candidate |
| runtime:canary:redfin_10803207:20260817_222851_c0551630:oc1_bd04d765dac55f9b | human_review | support | redfin_10803207 | photo_005.jpg | refuted | CAP-007:runtime:canary:redfin_10803207:20260817_222851_c0551630:oc1_bd04d765dac55f9b | unsupported | — |
| runtime:canary:redfin_25809814:20260817_223307_7b336e61:oc1_eac5f9ac3822499a | model_judge | context | redfin_25809814 | photo_023.jpg | refuted | CAP-007:runtime:canary:redfin_25809814:20260817_223307_7b336e61:oc1_eac5f9ac3822499a | unsupported | — |

**Evidence bar.** recomputed `two_independent_human` (claimed `two_independent_human`): 2 human/gold, 0 lead, 1 properties, 2 correlated, corroboration `none`, contextual `{"refuted":1}`; reviewed refutations 1 (lead 0), mixed signal `True`. Recounted by hand from the pinned adjudications. Three units carry role 'support'. Exactly two are adjudicated supports_claim: gold:redfin_11000447:photo_004.jpg:g7 and gold:redfin_11000447:photo_005.jpg:g4, both evidence_class gold_reference with independent true, both photos available. The third support unit (runtime:canary:redfin_10803207:...:oc1_bd04d765dac55f9b, human_review, case rc_a22a241b3bf0) is adjudicated refuted and therefore does not count. The fourth unit is role 'context' and evidence_class model_judge (the factorized lead on case rc_4f4b93c40ef3); it is excluded twice over and grounds nothing. So human = 2, leads = 0, and two or more human units gives two_independent_human. reviewed_refutations = 1 (rc_a22a241b3bf0, human, photos available), lead_refutations = 0, distinct_properties among counted units = 1. This matches digest/index.json (bar two_independent_human, human 2, lead 0, props 1, refutations 1, mixed_signal true) and the digest's evidence_bar_recomputed block exactly. Correlation: REQUIRED and supplied: both counted units are gold rows on the single property redfin_11000447, from one run (20260817_224602_d422d7ae), so distinct_properties = 1. They are not duplicates of one another - photo_004 is a kitchen window with a brown fabric valance plus taped winterizing sheeting, photo_005 is a bay-window bedroom with three sets of yellowed venetian blinds and explicitly no valance - so they are two different rooms, two different windows and two different treatment types, but they share one house's age and decorating habits and must not be read as two independent properties. Two counterweights are worth recording. First, the same property also supplies correct-rejection control rc_64f996e3a886 (its facade's vinyl replacement windows, Terra unsupported, reviewer refuted), so the property is not uniformly 'everything here is old'. Second, and more important, the correlated pair is NOT what the ops act on: the over-bill lane the split actually repairs is evidenced on four other and mutually distinct properties - redfin_10803207 (rc_a22a241b3bf0), redfin_25809814 (rc_4f4b93c40ef3), redfin_80925528 (rc_ffad088fa5fe) and redfin_11185681 (rc_429ba6851d09, the billed hallucination). The proposal therefore does not rest on the single-property pair alone, even though the formal bar does. Conflict: REQUIRED and supplied: mixed_signal is true because the support units contain both human/gold supports_claim (g7, g4) and a human/gold refutation (rc_a22a241b3bf0). The two directions are not in factual conflict - they are the same object conflation seen from opposite sides. The gold pair records real treatment-level conditions that the resolved item did not name (it named the window unit, or nothing); rc_a22a241b3bf0 records ordinary intact mini-blinds pulled onto a valance-committed item that Terra then correctly refused. Both say the item boundary sits in the wrong place. Note also that rc_a22a241b3bf0's older v1 truth (v1_verdict terra_claim_supported) disagrees with the Phase B reviewer, who refuted it; Steven's convention 6 reconciliation settles it explicitly - 'rc_a22a241b3bf0 ordinary mini-blinds = not billed' - so the Phase B adjudication and the convention agree, and the stale v1 label is superseded, not overturned by me.

**Controls.** 5 (4 required, missing 0, unreviewed family cards 0): rc_429ba6851d09 hallucination→refuted, rc_4f4b93c40ef3 correct_rejection→refuted, rc_64f996e3a886 correct_rejection→refuted, rc_7c5b9f9bb3aa positive_use→supports_claim

**Diagnosis.** primary `catalog_wording_or_specificity`; contributing retrieval_metadata_or_reachability, terra_verification, downstream_tier_projection_routing_package_costing, upstream_pass_2a_observation, upstream_kind_or_pass_2c. dated_window_treatment_valance is one item spanning two subjects whose billability Steven has ruled differently. Its Terra surface is the disjunction 'window treatments dated valance or treatments', whose second prong is satisfied by any window covering; its 2d/retrieval surface goes further and advertises 'Basic or builder-grade mini blinds ... Aged, bent, or damaged mini blinds', with support_any carrying the bare token 'blinds'. divergence_scan records exactly that gap: promised_not_claimed = [damage_break, plain_basic, wear_scuff]. Both surfaces are catalog fields, so under convention 1 this is catalog-owned, and the record shows it doing measurable damage. Five reviewed exposures of the item exist. Four are blinds-only and the Phase B reviewer refuted all four (rc_a22a241b3bf0, rc_4f4b93c40ef3, rc_ffad088fa5fe, rc_429ba6851d09). On two of them (rc_429ba6851d09, rc_ffad088fa5fe) resolution never reached the 2d LLM at all: resolution_path is lexical_shortcut with shortcut_reason support_phrase_hit, fired by the item's own support_any token 'blinds'. Terra then split on visually identical facts, returning unsupported three times ('no dated valance visible') and supported once - and that once, rc_429ba6851d09, ended accepted=true, disposition accepted_for_work, reason_code route_work. That is a billed hallucination whose entry point is a catalog keyword and whose survival is a catalog claim ambiguity. The fifth exposure, rc_7c5b9f9bb3aa, is the item's only human-approved billing and it turns on a fabric prong (a cloth hung as a makeshift curtain), which is the branch Steven left open. So the item is simultaneously over-reaching on blinds and carrying a genuine valance/curtain concept - the definition of a conflated item, and the shape a split resolves.

| rejected owner | why |
|---|---|
| pass_2d_selection | On the two LLM-path over-bill cases the model picked the rank-1 candidate and no better item existed on the frozen list - the reviewer says so for both ('No candidate fits'; dated_or_older_windows at rank 5 does not fit a blinds observation). On the other two over-bill cases 2d never chose at all: a catalog support_any token short-circuited it. Broadening or re-ranking cannot fix a list on which the correct answer does not exist. |
| upstream_pass_2a_observation | 2a is frozen and, for the over-bill lane, 2a is defensibly right: it reported blinds that really are basic ('The window and blinds are dated.', 'The blinds are older.'). What makes those unbillable is convention 3 and convention 5, which are catalog-side policy, not observation errors. The one genuine 2a miss in this cluster is g7's unnamed valance and winterizing sheeting, and that half is explicitly excluded from this proposal. |
| downstream_tier_projection_routing_package_costing | This IS the true owner of the two gold units' dollar loss, and the proposal does not claim otherwise. Both gold conditions were Terra-supported and then ended accepted=false / disposition no_action / reason_code route_no_action on dated_or_older_windows, which resolve_terminal_route reports as ('no_action','no_economics_approved_gap') because the item carries neither cost nor work_item_code. Economics are never authorable in the decisions file, so no catalog change recovers those dollars. It is recorded as a non-catalog follow-up; it is not the cluster's primary cause because it does not explain the over-bill lane the ops act on. |
| missing_catalog_coverage | The blinds subject is covered today - by an item that bills it. The defect is conflation, not absence, so this is a split of an existing item and not a coverage gap; see coverage_gap.rationale. |
| terra_verification | Terra's 3-1 inconsistency on identical blinds-only photos is real but derivative: every one of its rationales is a defensible reading of the string it was shown. Convention 1 assigns an unstable claim to the catalog surface that authored it. |

**Alternative-stage analysis.**

| question | answer |
|---|---|
| existing_item_exists | Yes. All 128 items were searched: dated_window_treatment_valance is the catalog's only window-treatment item and it already covers both blinds and valance/curtains; dated_or_older_windows covers the window unit itself. Nothing here is uncovered. There is no wear-kind window-treatment item, which is why g4's wear-classified blinds bullet found nothing. |
| upstream_kind_wrong | Partly, on one unit, and NOT fixed here. g4's bullet 'Window blinds are worn.' was carried as a wear/degradation-family observation and its entire frozen candidate list was wear items (door_hardware_worn, wall_scuffs_marks_or_dents, vinyl_linoleum_worn_or_stained, floor_dirty_or_heavily_soiled, peeling_or_discolored_paint, baseboard_wear_scuffs); dated_window_treatment_valance had rank null because it is modernization. Both proposed successors remain modernization, so that bullet would still resolve to nothing. Under convention 5 the blinds half is not billable anyway, so this is recorded, not repaired. For all four over-bill cards the kind was modernization and correct. |
| scene_or_retrieval_metadata_cause | Scene gating is not a cause: reachability_scan shows kind_ok and scene_ok true for both implicated items on every issue, and the item is valid in all five interior scene groups. Retrieval metadata IS a cause. support_any contains the bare token 'blinds', and two of five reviewed exposures resolved by lexical_shortcut / support_phrase_hit on it, bypassing LLM selection entirely (_resolve_candidate_via_lexical_shortcut in tools/scene_classifier_passes.py resolves the top candidate outright when any support term matches the observation). embed_text compounds it by advertising basic/worn/bent/damaged mini blinds - three concepts divergence_scan lists as promised_not_claimed. |
| retrieved_but_2d_selected_other | No for the over-bill lane; yes but harmless for one gold unit. On rc_a22a241b3bf0 and rc_4f4b93c40ef3 the LLM took rank-1 dated_window_treatment_valance and the reviewer records that no candidate on either list fits. On g7 the bullet 'The window appears older.' resolved by lexical_shortcut to dated_or_older_windows (rank 1) with dated_window_treatment_valance at rank 2, so the shortcut pre-empted an LLM choice that could have named the valance - but Terra supported the window reading and the reviewer confirms the wood window really is old, so no harm is demonstrated on that photo. It is filed as a pass_2d_follow_up, not as grounds for a change. |
| terra_adjudicated_wrong_detail | Yes on one card, and it is a symptom of the claim, not an independent Terra fault. Shown 'window treatments dated valance or treatments' against four visually equivalent blinds-only photos, Terra answered unsupported three times ('The windows show plain horizontal blinds, with no dated valance visible.') and supported once ('The window has visible horizontal blinds that appear dated.' - rc_429ba6851d09, which then billed). Both answers are defensible readings of a disjunction whose second prong any window covering satisfies. |
| wording_creates_unsupported_commitments | Yes, in both directions. The Terra surface commits to a valance-or-treatment subject with an unqualified 'dated' state, which cannot separate a builder-grade blind from a vintage valance; the retrieval surface commits to plain/basic, wear, and damage states that the atomic claim never states (divergence_scan promised_not_claimed = damage_break, plain_basic, wear_scuff). That mismatch between the two surfaces is exactly the convention-1 defect. |
| steals_neighbors_or_damages_successes | Neighbours: no. The frozen co-listed neighbours are dated_overall_decor_style, dated_or_older_windows, dated_wood_paneling, dated_interior_trim, dated_electrical_outlets_switches, ceiling_fan_blades_mismatched, mismatched_or_inconsistent_furniture_staging, outdated_bathroom_finishes, dated_lighting_fixtures, paint_refresh_recommended. Neither successor's text reaches those subjects, both keep the parent's scene_groups, and both retain the parent's glazing deny terms (broken window / fogged window / failed seal) so neither annexes glazing defects. Successes: one real exposure. rc_7c5b9f9bb3aa is the item's only human-approved billing and it is a MIXED photo (yellowed bent mini-blinds AND a red holiday-print cloth hung as a makeshift curtain). If 2d sends it to the blinds successor it is detected but routed no_action and the approved billing is lost. The blinds successor's deny_any on valance/curtain/drape terms is designed to force exactly that case to the fabric successor - deny_any is a hard guardrail matched against the observation text in CatalogEmbeddingsRetriever._passes_guardrails - but the card is a v1.1 label with no stored 2b bullet in the frozen bundle, so I cannot confirm offline that its observation names the cloth. This is the proposal's single largest risk and it is stated as such rather than assumed away. |
| alters_eligibility_package_economics | No. The dry run's added_detail shows both successors at pricing_status 'inherited_from_split_parent' with the parent's economics byte-identical: cost {mode: heuristic}, work_item_code WINDOW_TREATMENT_UPDATE, estimate null, cost_model null, package_affinity null, package_role 'ignore', estimate_scope null; tier stays optional, trade_bucket stays trim_doors_windows, and repair_support_marker_routes is empty for both, so neither drives nor supports a rehab package. Nothing is repriced and no economic field is authored - which is required, since economics are never authorable. The only economic consequence is routing: resolve_terminal_route returns ('no_action','route_override_no_action') for the blinds successor, making its inherited economics inert, and ('work','work_default') for the fabric successor, identical to the parent today. route_override is legal here precisely because the successor inherits a work_item_code - the validator rejects route_override on an item with neither cost nor work_item_code as redundant. |

**Semantic before after.** BEFORE - one item, dated_window_treatment_valance. Terra sees 'window treatments dated valance or treatments': a disjunction whose subject spans hard and fabric coverings and whose state is an unqualified 'dated', so a builder-grade mini-blind and a vintage valance are indistinguishable under it. 2d and retrieval see something broader still - embed_text 'Basic or builder-grade mini blinds, horizontal blinds, older blinds, worn window coverings ... Aged, bent, or damaged mini blinds' and support_any containing the bare token 'blinds'. One route: work. AFTER - two items with disjoint subjects. window_blinds_dated_or_damaged claims 'window blinds or shades / basic, aged, bent, or unevenly hung blinds' and owns all the blinds retrieval language, but carries route_override no_action, so a blinds finding is stated and displayed and never priced. dated_window_valance_or_curtains claims 'window valance or curtains / dated, heavy, or improvised fabric window treatment' and owns only the fabric language (valance, cornice, swag, curtains, drapes, sheers, and improvised cloth coverings), keeping the parent's work route. Each successor's Terra surface and retrieval surface now describe the same object, which is what convention 1 asks for and what the parent violated in three concepts (plain_basic, wear_scuff, damage_break).

**Retrieval effects.** Three mechanisms, of which two are deterministic and one is not. (1) DETERMINISTIC - the lexical shortcut. _resolve_candidate_via_lexical_shortcut resolves outright when any support_any term of the top candidate matches the observation. Today 'blinds' sits on the billing item, and that is literally how rc_429ba6851d09 (billed hallucination) and rc_ffad088fa5fe reached it. After the split every blinds token lives on the no_action successor, so the same shortcut, if it fires, lands on an item that cannot bill. (2) DETERMINISTIC - deny_any. It is a hard guardrail matched against the observation text in CatalogEmbeddingsRetriever._passes_guardrails; the blinds successor denies valance/curtain/curtains/drape/drapes/drapery, so any observation naming a fabric treatment cannot resolve to the no_action successor and is pushed to the billing one. Both successors keep the parent's glazing denies (broken window, fogged window, failed seal) so neither annexes glazing defects. (3) NOT VERIFIABLE OFFLINE - embedding rank. embed_text is the entire embedding input and there is no offline embedding store, so which successor ranks first for any given bullet, and whether the shortcut's score and margin thresholds (PASS_2D_SHORTCUT_MIN_SCORE 0.72, MIN_MARGIN 0.03) are still met once two similar items compete, can only be measured live. Note that two similar siblings can suppress the shortcut by shrinking the top-two margin, which would push these observations back to LLM selection - a neutral-to-good outcome, but an unmeasured one. Scene groups are unchanged on both successors (kitchen, bathroom, bedroom, living_areas, utility), so nothing new is reachable by scene.

**Economics fit.** No economic field is authored; economics are never authorable, and the dry run confirms none moved. Both successors take the split_successor_pricing policy 'inherit_parent_economics_v1' and come out with pricing_status 'inherited_from_split_parent', cost {mode: heuristic}, work_item_code WINDOW_TREATMENT_UPDATE, estimate null, cost_model null, package_affinity null, package_role 'ignore', estimate_scope null, tier optional, trade_bucket trim_doors_windows, category opportunity, display_class marketability - all inherited verbatim from dated_window_treatment_valance. The blinds successor's inherited economics are inert because route_override precedes the costing route. Retaining that work_item_code is not cosmetic: tools/catalog_validation.py rejects route_override on an item with neither cost nor work_item_code as redundant, so the inheritance is what makes the no_action override legal. Precedent is exact - landscaping_enhancement_opportunity is a split successor of landscape_improvement_needed carrying route_override no_action over an inherited LANDSCAPE_CLEANUP work code.

**Package route effects.** Routes, verified by running tools.renovation_architecture.catalog_projection.resolve_terminal_route on the generated items: window_blinds_dated_or_damaged -> ('no_action', 'route_override_no_action'), dated_window_valance_or_curtains -> ('work', 'work_default'), the latter identical to dated_window_treatment_valance today. Packages: package_role 'ignore' and package_affinity null are inherited by both, and the dry run reports repair_support_marker_routes empty for both, so neither successor drives a rehab package nor acts as contextual repair support under the 3.2 package_routing_decisions - the same position the parent holds. dated_or_older_windows is untouched and keeps its ('no_action','no_economics_approved_gap') route. Net billing change across the whole reviewed record: exactly one condition stops billing, rc_429ba6851d09, the reviewed hallucination.

**Product policy.** trade_bucket stays trim_doors_windows, which is not the quarantined trade (electrical), so no product-quarantine rule applies and nothing forces non_catalog_action. min_photo_evidence lives inside `estimate`, which is null on the parent and stays null on both successors, so no evidence-gate policy is touched. The proposal implements a stated product ruling (convention 5) rather than inventing one, and it deliberately implements only the blinds half of it, leaving valances and curtains billable exactly as Steven left them.

**Expected benefit.** Concrete and bounded. (1) The one reviewed billed hallucination on this item, rc_429ba6851d09 (Terra supported 'ordinary white mini-blinds, half raised and intact, with no valance'; accepted=true, disposition accepted_for_work, reason_code route_work), stops billing, because every blinds retrieval token now belongs to a no_action successor. On the reviewed record that halves this item's billings and removes the false one. (2) Convention 5 becomes true in the catalog rather than aspirational: blinds remain detectable and displayable, and are structurally unbillable. (3) Terra stops being asked an ambiguous question. The 3-1 split across visually equivalent blinds-only photos came from a disjunctive claim; each successor now commits to one object, so 'no dated valance visible' and 'blinds that appear dated' can no longer both be correct answers to the same string. (4) The fabric concept the humans actually approved (rc_7c5b9f9bb3aa's makeshift curtain, g7's brown valance) gets a claim and an embed text that name it, including improvised cloth coverings which the parent never mentioned. WHAT IT DOES NOT DO, stated plainly: neither gold unit becomes billed. Their loss is post-Terra routing onto dated_or_older_windows, an item with no economics by approved policy, and no catalog change reaches it. g4's wear-kind blinds bullet still resolves to no item. No dollars are recovered by this proposal; the benefit is one fewer false billing and a coherent boundary.

**Regression risk.** RESOLVED AGAINST THE PROPOSAL AS DRAFTED, by the lead, from the pinned run artifact. The author ranked rc_7c5b9f9bb3aa first and marked it unproven: the item's only human-approved billing (redfin_81000709 photo_003, condition oc1_3e3158279888272c, claim exact / work warranted / supported_billed / accepted_for_work), whose 2b bullet text is absent from the evidence bundle. Their decision rule was explicit - if the bullet names a cloth or curtain the blinds successor's deny_any diverts it and the split is safe; if it names only blinds, the approved billing is lost. I read the pinned, hash-verified artifact artifacts_canary/renovation_session9_20260818/run_1/candidate/redfin_81000709/20260817_225858_d13bd83c/photo_intel_debug.json (sha256 05738c0e..., matching the bundle pin, verified true). The bullet is 'The blinds are old.' - blinds only, no fabric term - so deny_any never fires and the condition lands on the no_action blinds successor. Terra's stored rationale is 'Yellowed horizontal blinds and an improvised window covering are visible.', disposition accepted_for_work, reason_code route_work, terminal route work. CONSEQUENCE: the split as drafted removes the item's only demonstrated correct billing. That loss is intended by convention 5 if the ruling covers these blinds, and is a genuine lost condition if it does not - see unresolved_questions. Secondary risk unchanged: embed_text is the entire embedding input and there is no offline embedding store, so every post-split ranking claim is a reasoned expectation rather than a measurement.

**Gap description.** Not the outcome - recorded because the analysis turned on it. The in-place variant of convention 5, leaving the id alone and putting route_override no_action on the existing carryover, is NOT natively expressible: carryover overrides are limited to name/description/embed_text/support_any/deny_any, and route_override sits in the inherited_fields set that only split successors may override (classify_op returns gap with 'carryover overrides are limited to wording fields; retrieval metadata, gates, and routes are not authorable here'). That is a genuine migration-system gap. It is not raised as the cluster's outcome for two reasons: the split reaches the same end natively, and the in-place version would be wrong on the merits anyway because it would route valances and curtains to no_action too, which Steven explicitly left open.

Worth watching because the same bare-token shortcut pattern also drove two of the over-bill cards on the other item. |

**Unresolved questions.** BLOCKING, and for Steven alone. Convention 5 says blinds stay detectable but must be a no_action catalog condition, and it was given against ordinary, clean, intact mini-blinds (rc_a22a241b3bf0). The item's only human-approved billing is also a blinds billing, but Terra's rationale there cites YELLOWED blinds - visible deterioration, not plain presence. Does the no_action ruling extend to visibly deteriorated blinds (yellowed, bent, unevenly hung), or only to ordinary plain ones? If it extends, this proposal is correct and the lost billing is convention 5 being applied. If it does not, the blinds successor needs a deterioration tell in its atomic_claim and must stay billable, and the proposal must be redrafted before implementation., The 2b bullet question the author raised is now ANSWERED and needs no live run: the bullet is 'The blinds are old.' See proposal.regression_risk for the artifact provenance., embed_text is the entire embedding input for an item and there is no offline embedding store, so the post-split ranks - which successor wins a given observation, and whether the lexical shortcut still fires on a blinds bullet at all - cannot be verified without a live retrieval run. Every retrieval claim here is reasoned, not measured.

### CAP-008 — Dated interior trim: the claim commits to plain/thin/builder-grade while every other surface of the item commits to datedness

Status `native_decisions_proposal` · outcome `native_decisions_proposal` · confidence `low` · items `dated_interior_trim`

**Claim under test.** The photo shows interior trim (door/window casing, jambs, wide or dark painted wood) that reads dated and aged, while it is not plain, thin, or builder-grade as the catalog atomic_claim commits.

**Cluster.** subject: interior trim package - door and window casing, jambs, and baseboards; mechanism: trim whose finish reads dated and aged (heavy dark-stained casings on cased openings, wide taupe-painted window casings with chipped uneven paint, aged taupe-painted panel doors and jambs), rejected by Terra because the atomic_claim commits only to a plain, thin, builder-grade material grade; kinds: modernization; scenes: living_areas, bedroom; commitments: scope. One item, one semantic inversion between two surfaces of that item, so no merge and no split. The cluster acquires sole ownership of dated_interior_trim: the family-structure step moved the redfin_11079485 shower-valve unit out of CAP-006 into CAP-021 because it is a plumbing-fixture subject resolved to an interior-millwork item, a wrong-subject-across-families mechanism rather than this item's wording inversion. That unit was the only carrier of dated_interior_trim into CAP-006; with it gone, CAP-006 releases the item and its seven dated_interior_trim control cards, all of which CAP-008 already held. Every worklist item CAP-006 and CAP-008 implicate today stays implicated, each by exactly one cluster.

**Mismatch class.** `operationally_consequential`; differing dimensions mechanism, billability.

| unit | class | role | property | photos | adjudication | review row | Terra | human truth |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| runtime:canary:redfin_80990371:20260817_221632_3d4a8269:oc1_c6c4c4017b1ed9dd | human_review | support | redfin_80990371 | photo_022.jpg, photo_028.jpg, photo_029.jpg | supports_claim | CAP-008:runtime:canary:redfin_80990371:20260817_221632_3d4a8269:oc1_c6c4c4017b1ed9dd | unsupported | exact |

**Evidence bar.** recomputed `structural_exception` (claimed `structural_exception`): 1 human/gold, 0 lead, 1 properties, 0 correlated, corroboration `none`, contextual `{}`; reviewed refutations 0 (lead 0), mixed signal `False`. Recounted by hand from the adjudications: one unit, role support, evidence_class human_review, independent true, adjudication supports_claim -> human = 1, leads = 0, distinct properties = 1, reviewed refutations = 0, mixed_signal false. The ladder therefore yields 'none', matching the digest's recomputed bar exactly. The only remaining route is structural_exception and it is available on all three requirements: (a) at least one human/gold counted unit exists (redfin_80990371, case rc_b642fe69b86a); (b) this is not a missing-coverage cluster, so the exception is legal; (c) the demonstration is a fact readable straight off the semantic snapshot rather than off the evidence - dated_interior_trim's name, description and embed_text commit to datedness while its atomic_claim commits only to material grade, corroborated by the divergence scan's promised_not_claimed = ['dated','door','window']. The exception is what licenses a proposal on one property; it is not a claim that one property proves a broad effect, which is why confidence is medium and why the live kill-condition check on rc_73d15ca14405 is named as a gate. Correlation: All support evidence is one condition on one property (redfin_80990371, condition oc1_c6c4c4017b1ed9dd, photos 022/028/029, three separate 2a observations projected onto that single condition - counted as one unit per convention 2, never multiplied). No second property corroborates the recovery direction. The suppression direction is separately evidenced by two hallucination controls on a different property (redfin_25809814) and the kill condition by a third (redfin_11185681), but those are controls, not support units, and they do not raise the bar. Conflict: There is no mixed human signal among the support units: the single support unit is adjudicated supports_claim, there are no refuted or unclear support units, so mixed_signal is false and no conflict_note is strictly required. The real tension lives in the controls and must not be hidden. Three positive_use cards carrying frozen v1.1 human truth claim exact / work warranted (rc_9e889631af67, rc_ceeb17f8e242, rc_d0ffde500a92) are described by the Phase B reviewer in almost the same words as the two hard_false_billed hallucinations (rc_022497229bd3, rc_a0e75a18620e): 'plain flat painted boards with no profile', 'narrow plain white baseboards, plain casing', 'a thin builder-grade trim, though clean and undamaged'. The visible facts do not separate the approved from the false, so any claim that requires visible datedness rejects all of them together. This proposal accepts that and argues it under convention 3 rather than pretending the hallucinations can be suppressed while the billings survive.

**Controls.** 7 (5 required, missing 0, unreviewed family cards 0): rc_022497229bd3 hallucination→supports_claim, rc_73d15ca14405 correct_rejection→refuted, rc_9e889631af67 positive_use→supports_claim, rc_a0e75a18620e hallucination→supports_claim, rc_ceeb17f8e242 positive_use→supports_claim

| convention 1 | Steven's governing conventions, Phase C brief section 2 | Strictest claim-bearing wording governs semantic fit; Terra sees only the atomic_claim while 2d and retrieval see name / description / embed_text / support_any, and a mismatch between those surfaces is a real catalog defect where it matters which surface failed. | Makes the surface divergence a nameable defect rather than a cosmetic inconsistency and identifies the atomic_claim as the surface that failed - which is why the op is on the atomic_claim alone and why the structural_exception fact is stated in terms of which surface commits to what. |

This is why the bar is 'none' on the ladder and why a structural exception is needed rather than claimed corroboration. |
| scope direction 2026-09-03 | Phase C brief section 3 | Catalog-first: consequential semantic defects are primary candidates, harmless naming differences get no_change plus documentation, and a 2d selection failure is never compensated by broadening the catalog. | Qualifies this cluster as in-scope - an incoherent claim that both loses a real condition and manufactures two false billings is not a harmless naming difference - and confirms no 2d compensation is involved, since 2d selected the right item on all three issues. |

**Diagnosis.** primary `catalog_wording_or_specificity`; contributing terra_verification. dated_interior_trim commits to two different conditions depending on which surface you read. Terra sees only the atomic_claim: subject 'interior trim', state 'plain, thin, or builder-grade trim package', ontology_basis low_grade_material - a claim about material grade. 2d and retrieval see name 'Dated or Basic Interior Trim', description 'Plain, thin, or dated interior trim package ... Builder-grade millwork suitable for modernization', and embed_text 'Dated or builder-grade interior trim ... older painted baseboards, dated window casing ... Trim modernization opportunity' - which promise datedness. The divergence scan independently records promised_not_claimed = ['dated','door','window'] for exactly this item. That split is why the same item both rejects genuinely dated substantial trim (rc_b642fe69b86a, human truth claim exact / work warranted) and accepts clean undamaged builder trim (rc_022497229bd3 and rc_a0e75a18620e, human truth claim absent / work none, both hard_false_billed, both Terra-supported): the retrieval surface pulls the plain-trim bullet in and the claim surface then certifies it. Both error directions are produced by the item's own wording with no upstream stage at fault, so ownership is the catalog's. Steven's reconciled ruling on rc_b642fe69b86a says this directly: the heavy dated trim warrants work even though the atomic claim is limited to plain/thin/builder-grade, and the wording mismatch is catalog-owned.

| rejected owner | why |
|---|---|

**Semantic before after.** BEFORE - atomic_claim {subject: 'interior trim', state: 'plain, thin, or builder-grade trim package', ontology_basis: 'low_grade_material'}, rendered to Terra as claim_text 'interior trim plain, thin, or builder-grade trim package'. The item asks Terra a question about material grade. AFTER - atomic_claim {subject: 'interior trim', state: 'dated trim package with an era-specific or aged finish, such as dark-stained, heavily painted-over, or worn painted casing, jambs, and baseboards; plain or period millwork in a clean current finish does not qualify', ontology_basis: 'dated_style'}. The item now asks Terra the question its name, description and embed_text have always advertised: does the trim finish read dated. ontology_basis moves low_grade_material -> dated_style, joining the 29 other modernization items already on that basis and matching house style (compare dated_wood_paneling 'dated wood paneling', older_flooring_style 'clean but visually outdated flooring', dated_or_older_windows 'dated style or aging window units, functional', vanity_countertop_dated 'dated pattern or material, intact'); qualifier clauses of this shape are already in-house on bathroom_paint_refresh_recommended ('intact but dated or dull paint') and vintage_tile_pattern_style ('vintage or dated pattern, intact'). Subject, kind, id, severity, scene_groups, retrieval text and every economic field are unchanged. Atomicity is preserved: one subject, one mechanism, the tail clause acting as an exclusion rather than a second claim.

**Retrieval effects.** None, by construction and confirmed by the dry run. embed_text, support_any, deny_any, require_any and scene_groups are untouched, and embed_text is non-empty so it remains the entire embedding input for this item - no rank moves anywhere, and the offline-unverifiable embedding surface is deliberately not touched. name and description are also untouched, so the 2d selection prompt sees exactly what it saw before and the frozen candidate lists stay valid: dated_interior_trim continues to appear and be selectable at ranks 3, 1 and 7 on the three support issues, and the co-listed neighbours (dated_overall_decor_style, dated_wood_paneling, dated_or_older_windows, dated_window_treatment_valance, dated_entry_or_patio_door, older_flooring_style, paint_refresh_recommended) are unaffected. The dry run's item_diff confirms the whole change is one field on one item: {'dated_interior_trim': ['atomic_claim']}, with added [], removed [] and item order unchanged. The residual, stated rather than hidden: because the retrieval text still advertises 'plain or basic baseboards', clean plain trim keeps being routed to this item and is now terminated at Terra instead of billed. That is the intended suppression path; the only cost is a Terra call that returns unsupported.

**Economics fit.** No economic field is authorable and none is touched. The item stays severity 1, tier optional, scope replace, trade_bucket trim_doors_windows, work_item_code TRIM_REPLACE, pricing_status null, cost_model null, min_photo_evidence untouched inside estimate. The fit improves rather than degrades: TRIM_REPLACE at tier optional is a modernization spend, which is the right economics for a trim package that reads dated and the wrong economics for certifying that clean, intact, undamaged builder trim exists - which is what the item does today on three of its four positive uses. Dollar direction on this evidence is downward (six uses lost, one recovered), which the brief treats as context only. Note the recovery is one condition, not three: the three 2a observations on redfin_80990371 all projected onto the single condition oc1_c6c4c4017b1ed9dd.

**Package route effects.** route_override stays null and drop_if_generic stays false; neither is authorable on a carryover in any case. package_affinity is unchanged and gives this item package_role 'package_support' in all four of bathroom_modernization, bedroom_modernization, kitchen_modernization and living_modernization - it is never a package driver. Support-only membership means a change in how often the item is accepted can strengthen or weaken an existing modernization package but can never create or destroy one, so no package comes into or goes out of existence from this change. Direction on this evidence: the redfin_80990371 living_areas condition becomes available as living_modernization support where it previously contributed nothing, while the redfin_11185681 kitchen and redfin_25809814 powder-room/bedroom supports drop out. kind stays modernization, so the 3.2 package_routing_decisions surface and the driver/support predicates are untouched.

**Product policy.** Not applicable. trade_bucket is trim_doors_windows, not the product-quarantined electrical trade, so no non_catalog_action is forced. The item carries no product lane, no pricing_status and no cost_model, and the estimate/min_photo_evidence gate is out of authoring reach and untouched. No product or gate policy is implicated.

**Expected benefit.** Two effects on billing correctness, stated with their full cost. (1) SUPPRESSION: the two required hallucination controls rc_022497229bd3 and rc_a0e75a18620e (redfin_25809814, human truth claim absent / work none, class hard_false_billed) are currently Terra-supported precisely because the claim asks about plain builder-grade material and the photos show plain builder-grade material that is clean and not dated. Under the new claim both should flip to rejected. These are not merely controls that survive a broadening - they are two false billings the current claim string actively causes, so the change removes a known hallucination mechanism rather than just failing to add one. (2) RECOVERY: the single support unit rc_b642fe69b86a (redfin_80990371, human truth claim exact / work warranted, class dirB_recovery) is a genuinely dated trim package that Terra correctly refused because the claim asked the wrong question; under the new claim it should be supported, recovering one real billable condition that three 2a observations, three exact 2b joins, 2c survival and a correct exact_kind 2d resolution had all carried intact. THE COST, stated plainly and not netted away: three positive_use cards whose frozen v1.1 human labels are claim exact / work warranted / supported_billed - rc_9e889631af67, rc_ceeb17f8e242 and rc_d0ffde500a92 - also flip to rejected, and a fourth, rc_57c6faf79c7e, flips from a supported billing on a frame containing no visible trim. This is NOT a clean win and must not be presented as one. The reviewer's own words show the visible facts do not separate the approved billings from the hallucinations: 'plain flat painted boards with no profile' (approved), 'narrow plain white baseboards, plain casing' (hallucination), 'a thin builder-grade trim, though clean and undamaged' (approved). Any claim that rejects the hallucinations rejects those billings too. The defence is convention 3 - plain/basic presence is not billable by itself - which post-dates those v1.1 labels and supersedes them: the labels were correct about the wording they were scored against, and that wording is no longer the standard. If the lead does not accept convention 3 as superseding those labels, this proposal should be overturned to no_change plus documentation of the field divergence.

**Regression risk.** Stated as a full ledger rather than a benefit case, because the reviewed record does not show a clean win. EXPECTED GAINS: the two hallucination cards (rc_022497229bd3, rc_a0e75a18620e) should flip to rejected, removing two known false billings of clean plain trim. EXPECTED LOSSES: the item's v1.1 labels include three approved billings the reviewer describes in almost the same words as the two hallucinations ('plain flat painted boards with no profile'; 'narrow plain white board ... clean and undamaged') - rc_9e889631af67, rc_ceeb17f8e242 and rc_d0ffde500a92 are all expected to flip to rejected, and rc_57c6faf79c7e (adjudicated unclear, no trim visible) is unpredictable. Those three losses are defensible ONLY as Steven's convention 3 (plain, basic or builder-grade presence is not billable by itself) superseding labels that predate the convention; they are not a bug being fixed and this proposal claims no credit for them. UNRESOLVED RISK: rc_73d15ca14405 is heavy period millwork in clean condition, correctly rejected today; an era-specific-finish claim could newly accept it, trading two suppressed hallucinations for one manufactured one. On Steven's own metric - fewer hallucinations AND fewer lost real conditions - this proposal is net-positive only if the period-trim collision does not fire. That single live check decides it.

**Gap description.** No coverage gap is declared. dated_interior_trim exists, covers the subject, sits in the right kind and scene_groups, appeared in the frozen candidate list on all three support issues and was actually selected by 2d on all three. The defect is intra-item surface incoherence, not absent coverage. This is also a mechanical necessity: a declared coverage gap would make the structural_exception route illegal, and with no legitimate split parent available (change_type reclassified, single successor) it would force the outcome to migration_system_gap instead of a fix.

**Unresolved questions.** BLOCKING before implementation: does the rewritten claim newly ACCEPT rc_73d15ca14405 (redfin_11185681 photos 006/044, heavy period trim in clean condition, correctly rejected today)? Period millwork is era-specific, so the claim's own tell may collide with a correct rejection. Only a live Terra call on those two photos settles it. If it fires, the proposal manufactures a hallucination and must not be implemented as drafted., House-style question for the human gate: the drafted state uses a semicolon and a negative exclusion clause. No atomic_claim in the 128-item catalog contains a negation, a semicolon or an exclusion clause (maximum state length is nine words). Either the clause is accepted as a deliberate first, or the state is rewritten positive-only, which removes the guard against the period-trim collision and makes the live check decisive rather than merely prudent., Accepted consequence for the human gate to confirm: three human-approved billings (rc_9e889631af67, rc_ceeb17f8e242, rc_d0ffde500a92) are expected to stop billing. That is convention 3 being applied to labels that predate it, not a defect being repaired. If Steven does not want those billings withdrawn, the proposal falls and the item's claim-versus-name divergence should be recorded as documentation only.

**Human disposition.** _blank_ (approved / approved_with_modification / rejected / deferred / reclassified_non_catalog)

## Non-catalog actions

### CAP-001 — Interior paint and trim: mechanism-specific claims vs tired/aged/worn finishes

Status `non_catalog_action` · outcome `non_catalog_action` · confidence `high` · items `baseboard_wear_scuffs`, `peeling_or_discolored_paint`

**Claim under test.** The photo shows an interior painted surface (wall, baseboard/trim, or painted brick) whose finish is visibly tired, aged, uneven, patchy, or discolored in a way a buyer would notice, while the specific mechanism the catalog claim names (peeling/bubbling for peeling_or_discolored_paint; scuffs, paint loss, dents for baseboard_wear_scuffs) is not clearly visible.

**Cluster.** subject: interior painted surfaces: walls, baseboards/trim, painted brick; mechanism: finish looks tired, aged, uneven, patchy, or discolored without a clearly visible named mechanism (peeling, bubbling, scuffs, dents, paint loss); kinds: degradation; scenes: kitchen, bathroom, bedroom, living_areas; commitments: mechanism, severity. Kept exactly as reviewed: pooled, no unit moved, no split, no merge, no coverage unit promoted, and the claim_under_test is carried verbatim because the reviewer adjudicated seven rows against those exact words. Pooling is right because every unit is one interior painted surface whose resolved item commits to a named mechanism the observation did not assert, and because the two implicated items share kind (degradation), severity (1), tier (work), scope (cosmetic) and package_role (package_support), so they fail the same way. Splitting was considered and rejected on arithmetic as well as on shape: the family allocated no new proposal ids, and peeling_or_discolored_paint alone would keep 2 counted supports on 2 properties while a baseboard-only cluster would hold 1 support at bar one_human_plus_independent_corroboration-or-none, and splitting the single 2d-specificity unit (f46cc039) off would leave a one-unit cluster at bar none. One correction to the provisional cluster block, factual and not a re-scoping: the scenes list omitted kitchen, which is the scene of unit f46cc039 (case rc_4cab6e7be02b); kitchen is added here.

**Mismatch class.** `operationally_equivalent`; differing dimensions —.

The three supports also span three scenes (kitchen, living_areas, living_areas fireplace) and both implicated items, so the bar is not carried by a single item. Conflict: Mixed signal is real and mandatory to state: three human supports_claim and three human refuted adjudications sit inside this cluster. Supports: f46cc039 (galley-kitchen walls blotchy, patched and scuffed with no peeling film), d7af7b65 (wide painted baseboard grimy and tired with no resolvable scuffs or dents), 633bb1ea (painted brick surround thin and streaky with brick and mortar showing through, no lifting paint). Refutations: b10d0b13 and e26ef990, both role=support, where the reviewer found the named mechanism plainly visible - bare, dark, paint-absent base trim in the half-bath, and a diagonal crack with lifted paint edges and flaking beside the bedroom door casing - so the cluster claim's 'mechanism not clearly visible' premise fails on those photos; and counterexample 4a9736e4, a uniform clean butter-yellow dining room with human truth absent/none. The conflict does not cancel the bar because it splits along a line that reinforces the outcome: on b10d0b13 and e26ef990 the human truths are exact/warranted and Terra still rejected, so those photos are additional Terra false rejections of claims that already fitted, while 4a9736e4 is a Terra false acceptance on intact paint. In other words the same wording fails in both directions on the same evidence, which is precisely why no wording change is proposed. No adjudication is contested here; the divergences from frozen v1.1 truth that do exist are listed as unresolved questions.

**Diagnosis.** primary `terra_verification`; contributing upstream_kind_or_pass_2c, pass_2d_selection, overlapping_or_ambiguous_ontology. The catalog does not own this cluster, and the evidence closes the catalog lever from both sides at once. Both atomic claims already contain the disjunct that covers the supported photos: baseboard_wear_scuffs states 'scuffs, paint loss, dents, or general wear' and peeling_or_discolored_paint states 'peeling, bubbling, or visibly aged finish'. That same broad disjunct is simultaneously (a) the clause Terra refused to test on four real conditions the humans graded exact/warranted (b10d0b13, d7af7b65, e26ef990, 633bb1ea; all four carry disposition excluded / reason_code verdict_unsupported) and (b) the clause Terra leaned on to bill two intact surfaces (in-cluster counterexample 4a9736e4, truth absent/none, plus hallucination control rc_ab90ab9da2ab, truth absent/none). One phrase cannot be widened to rescue the four false rejections and narrowed to block the two false acceptances. More decisively, the cluster's own evidence shows that wording precision is not the binding lever at all: on d7af7b65 Terra wrote 'generally intact without clear scuffs, paint loss, or dents' and never evaluated 'general wear', on 633bb1ea it wrote 'no peeling or bubbling paint is apparent' and never evaluated 'visibly aged finish', and on 4a9736e4 it asserted 'visibly uneven, discolored areas and an aged-looking finish' about a uniform clean wall. A verifier that skips disjuncts it was given and invents detail it was not given will do the same to any replacement wording, so a re-worded claim cannot be predicted to move either error class. The catalog-owned facts that do exist here are real but inconsequential in this evidence: the item name and description over-commit to mechanism ('Peeling or Discolored Paint'; 'Paint that is peeling, bubbling, cracked, or visibly aged') while Terra only ever sees the atomic claim, and both items' embed_text promises more than their claim (divergence_scan: peeling_or_discolored_paint promised_not_claimed damage_break, moisture_rot, stain_discolor; baseboard_wear_scuffs promised_not_claimed missing_incomplete, trim_casing). Neither divergence changed a billing outcome in any of these seven units, so under the 2026-09-03 catalog-first direction they get documentation, not a change.

**Semantic before after.** No change. peeling_or_discolored_paint keeps atomic_claim subject 'interior paint' / state 'peeling, bubbling, or visibly aged finish' (ontology_basis visible_deterioration) and baseboard_wear_scuffs keeps subject 'baseboards' / state 'scuffs, paint loss, dents, or general wear' (ontology_basis dent_without_functional_impact). Both remain change_type reclassified carryovers with a single successor each. Nothing in the decisions file is touched, so the migration output is byte-identical.

**Retrieval effects.** None: no embed_text, name, description, support_any, deny_any or require_any is edited, so no item's rank changes anywhere and no offline-unverifiable embedding edit is incurred. Recorded for the lead's documentation lane: both items already promise more in retrieval than their claims deliver (peeling_or_discolored_paint promised_not_claimed damage_break, moisture_rot, stain_discolor - its embed advertises 'Lower-wall moisture staining, tide marks'; baseboard_wear_scuffs promised_not_claimed missing_incomplete, trim_casing - its embed advertises 'Missing baseboards, detached trim, visible gaps at wall base'). That is the channel by which incompleteness and staining observations reach claims that cannot cover them, and it is the substance of unresolved question 1.

**Economics fit.** No economics field is touched; none is natively authorable for a carryover in any case. Context only: both items are severity 1, tier work, scope cosmetic, package_role package_support with repair_support_when_driven true across bathroom/bedroom/kitchen/living, so the six human units in this cluster differ from one another in name and trade bucket, not in materiality. Dollar effects of the four Terra false rejections are context under the scope direction and are not used to justify anything.

**Package route effects.** None. route_override stays null on both items (no no_action route is implicated), trade buckets stay paint_drywall and trim_doors_windows, work item codes stay PAINT_INTERIOR and TRIM_REPAIR, and package_affinity is unchanged. The non-catalog actions proposed below are all verifier- or upstream-stage actions and cannot move a route by themselves.

**Product policy.** Not applicable. Neither implicated item is in a product-quarantined trade; electrical is not involved, so nothing forces a product-policy outcome.

**Expected benefit.** The benefit is claimed for the non-catalog actions, not for a catalog change. (1) Terra calibration - require the verifier to evaluate every disjunct of the atomic claim it is given, and to refuse to assert visual detail the image does not show. On this cluster alone that addresses four false rejections of conditions the humans graded exact/warranted (b10d0b13, d7af7b65, e26ef990, 633bb1ea) and two false acceptances on intact paint (4a9736e4 and control rc_ab90ab9da2ab), i.e. errors in both directions from one calibration lever, which is exactly what a wording lever cannot do. (2) 2c kind routing - a dated-hue observation such as 'Wall color is yellowed' must be kinded modernization so paint_refresh_recommended (tier optional) becomes reachable; today it is kinded degradation and the modernization item is gated out with rank null on every unit here, which is the mechanism of the yellow-wall hard_false_billed card. (3) One pass_2d_follow_ups entry (f46cc039). (4) Documentation of the name/description and embed-vs-claim divergences on both items, per the scope direction's harmless-naming rule.

**Regression risk.** Zero from this finding, because there are no ops and the guarded files are untouched. The risk being deliberately avoided is stated in the negative: a broadening of either claim would be tested against five confirmed correct rejections (rc_42f54b0e00fd, rc_532fa2016ed9, rc_7380e1876137, rc_aeea1143efaf, rc_cc24e226f76c) and two hallucination controls (in-cluster 4a9736e4, card rc_ab90ab9da2ab), and a narrowing would be tested against two of the three counted supports (f46cc039, 633bb1ea) and three positive-use cards whose reviewer reasons rest on the very disjunct that would be removed (rc_025ca614449b, rc_49b3ff4a6be8, rc_d58b1f530fd9). The Terra-calibration action carries its own regression exposure that must be measured live rather than here: making the verifier enumerate disjuncts could raise acceptance on marginal surfaces, so the five correct-rejection cards plus rc_ab90ab9da2ab should be re-run as the control set for that action.

| rc_4cab6e7be02b | peeling_or_discolored_paint | wall_scuffs_marks_or_dents | 6 | paint_film_vs_wall_surface_wear | Confusion family, stated in full: paint-film failure vs wall-surface wear: two degradation items in the same trade (paint_drywall) and tier (work) at severity 1, differing in work_item_code (PAINT_INTERIOR vs DRYWALL_PATCH) and in whether the defect is the coating or the substrate redfin_10806500 photo_004, kitchen scene, resolution_path llm, routing_reason exact_kind. 2d mapped the observation 'Wall finishes appear tired.' to peeling_or_discolored_paint at rank 7 while wall_scuffs_marks_or_dents sat at rank 6 with kind_ok and scene_ok both true; the 2a prose named the correct reading and only the correct reading ('Visible scuffs, patchy areas, and generally tired finishes suggest prep, repair, and repainting throughout') and the sibling issue on the same photo did resolve to wall_scuffs_marks_or_dents, so the better item was reachable and demonstrably selectable in the same run. The reviewer's own lineage note agrees: 'wall_scuffs_marks_or_dents (rank 6) fits the scuffs and patched areas better than a peeling item'. Human truth is misnamed/warranted and the condition was billed (accepted_for_work), so the correction is naming and specificity only: identical kind, severity, tier, scope and package_role, no economics delta. **Unresolved questions.** baseboard_wear_scuffs promises incompleteness in retrieval that its claim does not cover. Its embed_text reads 'Missing baseboards, detached trim, visible gaps at wall base, incomplete base trim installation' and divergence_scan reports promised_not_claimed [missing_incomplete, trim_casing], while the atomic claim Terra sees is only 'scuffs, paint loss, dents, or general wear'. Unit b10d0b13's 2a observation was exactly an incompleteness observation ('Baseboards, caulking, and paint lines look incomplete or rough') and Terra rejected it on wear vocabulary. This is a genuine catalog-owned surface split, but it has zero supporting adjudications today because that unit is adjudicated refuted, so it cannot ground anything. Promotion recipe: human review of two photos on distinct properties showing missing or incomplete base trim where the pipeline resolved baseboard_wear_scuffs; two supports_claim verdicts would open a properly evidenced claim-coverage question., Truth conflict on control rc_924163d5a9c3: frozen v1.1 truth is exact/warranted and Terra said supported, but the Phase B reviewer refuted it on the photos. Until reconciled it should not be leaned on as either a success control or an over-billing control. Flagged only; the adjudication stands as recorded and is not changed here., Whether a differently worded claim would change Terra's behaviour is untestable offline - there is no Terra replay in this evidence set - and the direct evidence here (three units where Terra skipped a disjunct it was given, two where it asserted detail the photo lacks) argues it would not. Any future wording hypothesis on these two items needs a live Terra arm before it can be evaluated., peeling_or_discolored_paint's degradation claim ('visibly aged finish', tier work) and paint_refresh_recommended's modernization retrieval text ('Interior paint looks aged, yellowed ... or dated', tier optional) both claim the word 'aged' across the kind boundary. That overlap is what lets an intact dated colour land on a work-tier degradation claim. The lever is the 2c kind decision, not either claim, but if a future cluster establishes the overlap costs billings in both directions it may deserve its own proposal - it cannot be raised here because the only unit that exercises it is the refuted counterexample., f46cc039's kitchen candidate list put five kitchen-object items ahead of both wall items (wall_scuffs_marks_or_dents 6, peeling_or_discolored_paint 7). Whether retrieval systematically under-ranks wall and trim items in kitchen scenes is unmeasured - this cluster has a single kitchen unit - and would need a scan across kitchen-scene units to become a finding.

### CAP-003 — Dated paint palette: neutral colours the human called dated, Terra called not personalized

Status `non_catalog_action` · outcome `non_catalog_action` · confidence `high` · items `paint_refresh_recommended`

**Claim under test.** The photo shows interior wall/ceiling paint in a neutral but visibly dated palette (beige, tan, dated gray or scheme) that a buyer would repaint, while nothing in the room is a highly personalized or loud colour scheme.

**Cluster.** subject: interior wall and ceiling paint colour; mechanism: neutral beige/tan/gray palette that reads dated to the reviewer but is not highly personalized; kinds: modernization; scenes: bedroom, living_areas, kitchen; commitments: scope. Kept exactly as reviewed: one item, four human units on four distinct properties, one semantic question (does 'dated' cover a neutral-but-tired palette) spanning all four, and the claim_under_test kept verbatim because the reviewer adjudicated against those exact words. No split: the four units differ only in how strong their era tell is, not in subject, mechanism, item, kind, scene family or commitment type, and splitting the two weaker units off would leave a two-unit cluster and a two-unit cluster where a single four-unit cluster answers the same question with a stronger bar. No merge: CAP-016 (ceiling texture) shares the dated_style ontology basis but is a different physical subject, a different item and an object commitment rather than a colour/scope commitment, and it is lead-only, so merging would contaminate a clean four-human palette question. No unit imported from coverage and no unit exported: paint_refresh_recommended remains implicated only here.

**Mismatch class.** `exact_match`; differing dimensions —.

The ruling also dictates the SHAPE of the recommended Terra calibration - tell-based, never hue-based - and it is what makes the three refuted controls (current blue-grey, current greige, plain mid-grey) correct rejections rather than misses. |

**Diagnosis.** primary `terra_verification`; contributing catalog_wording_or_specificity. Catalog ownership was tested and rejected. Under convention 1 the surface that decides Terra's verdict is the atomic_claim alone, and that claim already reads subject 'interior paint color', state 'dated or highly personalized colors' - a disjunction whose first limb is exactly what all six 2a observations asserted ('Wall and ceiling paint is dated', 'The wall paint is beige/tan and dated', 'The paint scheme is dated', 'The paint palette is dated', 'Room has a dated paint palette and finishes', 'The beige wall paint is dated'). Terra rejected all four cases on the second limb without ever adjudicating the first: 'neutral and not clearly dated or highly personalized' (f5459a09), 'neutral and not clearly highly personalized or dated' (6ea2a14d), 'neutral beige color without a clearly highly personalized color scheme' (29c5c08e, which concedes the beige outright), 'neutral and not clearly dated or highly personalized' (8f96473f). A disjunct that is present in the claim and unread by the verifier is not a wording defect that the catalog can fix; there is no rewrite of a claim that already says 'dated' that makes 'dated' more present. divergence_scan independently reports paint_refresh_recommended with an empty promised_not_claimed and an empty claimed_not_promised list - retrieval text and claim text agree, so the retrieval-vs-claim split that drives CAP-014 and CAP-016 does not exist on this item. The one honest catalog-side observation is recorded as a contributing cause only: 'highly personalized colors' is the concrete, testable half of the disjunction while 'dated' is left unqualified, and all four rationales anchor on the concrete half. Acting on that observation - inserting convention-4 tells ('era-specific builder beige/tan, worn or yellowed finish, dated colour scheme') into the atomic_claim - is natively expressible (paint_refresh_recommended is a reclassified carryover, so /successors/0/atomic_claim is settable), but it is NOT warranted on this evidence: the item is already over-firing in the opposite direction on the very same claim (control rc_4abeff09724f, redfin_10949071 photo_006, a required positive_use where Terra said 'supported' on freshly painted current blue-grey and the Phase B reviewer adjudicated refuted), and two more controls (rc_ef3234842cb9 mid-grey, rc_c34e48007ce2 current greige) are correct rejections that any loosening of 'dated' puts at risk. Broadening a claim that is simultaneously being read too narrowly by the verifier and too loosely on clean walls would trade four recovered conditions for an unknown number of new hallucinations, which is the opposite of the scope direction's judging criterion. The lever that actually moves these four cases sits in Terra's reading of the claim, not in the catalog.
